Ma'rifat</div></div></div></div><div class="rounded-[20px] bg-[#ECFDF5] border-2 border-emerald-200 overflow-hidden"><div class="px-5 py-3 bg-white border-b border-emerald-200 flex items-center Lihat Tanbih</p></div></div></div></section><section class="rounded-[24px] bg-[#EFF6FF] border-2 border-blue-300 overflow-hidden mb-6 shadow-sm"><div class="px-5 md:px-7 py-4 bg-white border-b border-blue-200 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-triangle-alert w-4 h-4"><path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3"></path><path d="M12 9v4"></path><path d="M12 17h.01"></path></svg></div><h2 class="font-bold text-[15px] text-blue-900">4. Itu tasybih &amp; hulul - BATAL!</p></div></div><div class="bg-white rounded-[16px] border border-blue-200 p-4"><p class="font-bold text-[13px] text-blue-900 mb-3">MAKSUD YANG BENAR:</p><div class="space-y-2 text-[14px]"><div class="flex gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200"><span class="font-bold">قَبْلَ</span><span>= secara martabat wujud, Allah lebih dulu (Qidam)</span></div><div class="flex gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200"><span class="font-bold">بَعْدَ</span><span>= secara dalil, kita tahu alam dulu baru istidlal ke Allah</span></div><div class="flex gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200"><span class="font-bold">فِيْهِ</span><span>= syuhud tajalli sifat Allah pada alam, bukan Allah masuk alam</span></div><div class="flex gap-2 p-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200"><span class="font-bold">عِنْدَ</span><span>= syuhud qurb ma'nawi, bukan jarak fisik</span></div></div><p class="text-[12px] mt-3 text-zinc-600">Zaman &amp; makan itu sendiri = bagian dari اَلْأَكْوَانِ → tidak mungkin Allah terikat dengannya</p></div></div></div></section><section class="rounded-[24px] bg-[#FFFBEB] border-2 border-orange-300 overflow-hidden mb-6 shadow-sm"><div class="px-5 md:px-7 py-4 bg-white border-b border-orange-200 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-orange-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-triangle-alert w-4 h-4"><path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3"></path><path d="M12 9v4"></path><path d="M12 17h.01"></path></svg></div><h2 class="font-bold text-[15px]">5. PERINGATAN ZALLAT AQDOM - BANYAK KAKI TERGELINCIR</h2><span class="ml-auto text-[10px] px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-orange-500 text-white font-bold">HATI-HATI!</span></div><div class="p-5 md:p-7"><p class="font-arab text-[22px] leading-[2] text-[#78350f] text-right bg-white rounded-[16px] border border-orange-300 p-5">وَمَعْرِفَةُ تَفْصِيْلِ هَذِهِ الْأُمُوْرِ وَالتَّفْرِقَةِ بَيْنَ هَذِهِ الْحَقَائِقِ عَلَى مَا هِيَ عَلَيْهِ مَوْكُوْلٌ إِلَى أَرْبَابِهِ، فَلْنَقْتَصِرْ عَلَى مَا ذَكَرْنَاهُ فَهَاهُنَا زَلَّتْ أَقْدَامُ كَثِيْرٍ مِنَ النَّاسِ فَتَكَلَّمُوْا بِكَلِمَاتٍ مُوْهِمَةٍ، وَعَبَّرُوْا بِعِبَارَاتٍ مُنْكَرَةٍ فِي الشَّرْعِ فَكَفَّرُوْا بِذَلِكَ، وَبُدِّعُوْا</p><div class="mt-4 grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-3"><div class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-orange-200 p-4 md:col-span-2"><p class="text-[15px] leading-[1.7]"><b>Artinya mudah:</b> Pengetahuan rinci ini diserahkan pada ahlinya, cukup sampai sini, <b>di sini banyak kaki tergelincir</b>, bicara kata-kata membingungkan &amp; ibarat mungkar dalam syara' lalu kafir &amp; bid'ah</p><div class="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-[13px]"><div class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-red-50 border border-red-200"><b>Contoh salah:</b> "Allah menyatu dengan alam" → kufur</div><div class="p-2 rounded-lg bg-red-50 border border-red-200"><b>Contoh salah:</b> "Alam adalah Allah" (wahdat wujud salah) → bid'ah</div></div></div><div class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-orange-200 p-4 flex flex-col justify-center"><p class="text-[12px] font-bold text-orange-800">SIKAP YANG BENAR:</p><p class="text-[14px] mt-1 leading-[1.6]">Cukup sampai sini, serahkan tafshil pada <b>arbabuh (ahlinya)</b> yang sudah matang ma'rifat &amp; syariat. Jangan sok dalam kalau belum matang!</p></div></div></div></section><section class="rounded-[24px] bg-white border-2 border-emerald-400 overflow-hidden shadow-[0_8px_30px_rgba(16,185,129,0.15)]"><div class="px-5 md:px-7 py-4 bg-[#ECFDF5] border-b border-emerald-200 flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-heart w-4 h-4"><path d="M19 14c1.49-1.46 3-3.21 3-5.5A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 16.5 3c-1.76 0-3 .5-4.5 2-1.5-1.5-2.74-2-4.5-2A5.5 5.5 0 0 0 2 8.5c0 2.3 1.5 4.05 3 5.5l7 7Z"></path></svg></div><h2 class="font-bold text-[15px] text-emerald-900">AQIDAH SELAMAT - KUNCI AKHIR HIKMAH 14</h2><span class="ml-auto text-[10px] px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold">TANZIH MUTLAQ</span></div><div class="p-5 md:p-8"><div class="rounded-[16px] bg-[#FFFBEB] border border-amber-300 p-5 md:p-6 text-center"><p class="font-arab text-[26px] leading-[1.9] text-[#78350f]">فَاعْتَقِدْ كَمَالَ التَّنْزِيْهِ وَبُطْلَانَ التَّشْبِيْهِ، وَتَمَسَّكْ بِقَوْلِهِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ: لَيْسَ كَمِثْلِهِ شَيْءٌ، وَهُوَ السَّمِيْعُ الْبَصِيْرُ <span class="text-[16px]">[الشورى 11]</span> لَا إِلَهَ غَيْرُهُ</p></div><div class="mt-5 grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4"><div class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#fbbf24] p-4"><p class="text-[15px] leading-[1.7]"><b>Terjemah mudah:</b> Yakinlah kesempurnaan tanzih &amp; tidak mahjub • Yang tidak mahjub ada 4 maqam: Qabla, Ba'da, Fihi, 'Indahu • Semua zharaf bukan zaman/makan &amp; ittisal/infishal bukan makna dhahir • Banyak tergelincir di sini → pegang <b>لَيْسَ كَمِثْلِهِ شَيْءٌ</b></p></div></div></section><footer class="mt-10 text-center"><div class="inline-flex flex-col items-center gap-2 px-6 py-4 rounded-[16px] bg-white border border-amber-200 shadow-sm"><p class="font-arab text-[18px] text-[#78350f]">تم بحمد الله - Hikmah 14 Syarah Ibnu Abbad</p><p class="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">Latar terang #FFFBEB • Card putih #FFFFFF • Border emas #fbbf24 • Teks #1f2937 • Arab #78350f 26px • Penjelasan 15px • Visual lengkap 100%</p></div></footer></main></div></div>
